P3·DEV·4 Dev không update ticket trạng thái Dev does not update ticket status #

Ticket hygieneTicket hygiene💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 5 phút⏱ 5 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Board lệch thực tế: 3 ticket đã merge nhưng vẫn ở "In Progress". Dev: "em đang focus code, sau update".
Actual deviation of the board: 3 tickets have been merged but are still in "In Progress". Dev: "I'm focusing on the code, after the update".
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
EM tự move ticket cho dev → maintenance forever, không bao giờ tự sửa.
I move the ticket to the dev myself → maintenance forever, never fix it myself.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh hiểu em focus code — nhưng board lệch thực tế là noise phá team: • Anh không biết workload thật → assign mới sai. • PO nhìn dashboard panic không cần thiết. • Standup tốn thời gian "nhắc lại trạng thái thật". Quy tắc đơn giản: 1 hành động = 1 update: • Bắt đầu task → drag sang "In Progress" + ghi 1 dòng plan. • Mở PR → drag sang "Review" + paste link. • PR merged → drag sang "Done" + ghi link deploy. • Block → drag sang "Blocked" + ghi blocker là gì. Tổng thời gian: ~30 giây/ngày. Trade-off cực lợi. Nếu em không có thói quen, anh add bước này vào DoD: ticket không update = story không done dù code đã merge.
I understand your focus code — but the actual board is wrongnoise. noise break the team: • You don't know the real workload → assign is wrong. • PO looks at dashboard and panics unnecessarily. • Standup takes time to "recall the true state". Simple rules:1 action = 1 update: • Start task → drag to "In Progress" + write 1 line of plan. • Open PR → drag to "Review" + paste link. • PR merged → drag to "Done" + write deploy link. • Block → drag to "Blocked" + write what blocker is. Total time: ~30 seconds/day. Trade-off is extremely beneficial. If you are not in the habit, I add this step to DoD: ticket not updated = story not done even though the code has been merged.
💡 Lưu ý: Ticket hygiene = team thông tin chung. EM không nên tự move thay → enforce ownership.💡 Note: Ticket hygiene = team general information. You should not move yourself instead → enforce ownership.

P3·DEV·5 Dev tham gia retro nửa vời, không action item Dev participated in the retro half-heartedly, without action items #

Action-driven retroAction-driven retro💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 10 phút⏱ 10 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Retro lặp 4 sprint cùng vấn đề ("CR review chậm"), không ai own action item.
Retro repeated 4 sprints with the same problem ("CR review is slow"), no one owns the action item.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Coi retro là "venting session" → vấn đề lặp lại mãi → team mất tin retro.
Consider retro as "venting session" → problem repeats forever → team loses retro news.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh thay đổi format retro từ tuần này: • 3 cột: Start / Stop / Continue — 5 phút mỗi người. • Vote chọn top 2 issue để fix. • Mỗi issue: 1 action item, 1 owner, 1 deadline. Không có owner = action không tồn tại. • Retro mở đầu sprint sau: review action item từ retro trước. Done? Helped? Killed? Cụ thể "CR review chậm" lặp 4 sprint: • Anh propose: review buddy rotation — mỗi PR có 1 designated reviewer trong 24h. • Owner: tech lead. Deadline: cuối sprint tới. • Sprint sau retro: đo lại cycle time review. Retro không action = than thở. Anh muốn team trả 60 phút retro → save 5+ giờ/sprint.
He changed the retro format from this week: • 3 columns:Start / Stop / Continue— 5 minutes per person. • Vote for your choicetop 2 issues to fix. • Each issue:1 action item, 1 owner, 1 deadline. No owner = action does not exist. • Retro opens the next sprint: review action items from the previous retro. Done? Helped? Killed? Specifically, "Slow CR review" repeated over 4 sprints: • He proposes:review buddy rotation— Each PR has 1 designated reviewer for 24 hours. • Owner: tech lead. Deadline: end of next sprint. • Sprint after retro: measure cycle time review again. Retro without action = lament. He wants the team to pay for 60 retro minutes → save 5+ hours/sprint.
💡 Lưu ý: Mỗi retro < 3 action item, mỗi action có owner. Track cross-sprint, không "drop and forget".💡 Note: Each retro < 3 action items, each action has an owner. Track cross-sprint, not "drop and forget".

⚠️ P4 — Risk & Issue Management (15)

Phát hiện sớm, log đầy đủ, mitigation rõ ràng, escalation đúng kênh. Risk = chưa xảy ra, có thể phòng. Issue = đã xảy ra, phải xử.

🎯 Kỹ thuật chủ đạo

📋

RAID Log

Risks · Assumptions · Issues · Dependencies. 1 sheet sống suốt project.

Dùng khi: kickoff + weekly
🎯

Risk Matrix (P × I)

Probability (1–5) × Impact (1–5). Score > 12 = phải có owner + mitigation cụ thể.

Dùng khi: prioritize risk
🛡️

Mitigation vs Contingency

Mitigation: giảm xác suất xảy ra. Contingency: làm gì nếu xảy ra. Phải có cả 2.

Dùng khi: risk > 12
📈

Pre-mortem

"Giả sử project fail sau 3 tháng — vì sao?" — list 10 nguyên nhân ngay từ đầu, ưu tiên top 3.

Dùng khi: kickoff
🚨

Escalation Ladder

Lvl 1 dev → Lvl 2 lead → Lvl 3 EM → Lvl 4 director. Mỗi cấp có SLA: 1d, 2d, 3d. Không skip cấp.

Dùng khi: blocker dài
🧯

Blameless Postmortem

Khi issue xảy ra: tập trung hệ thống, không tập trung người. 5 Whys → action item, không tìm tội.

Dùng khi: sau incident
Pre-mortem→ RAID log→ Risk matrix→ Mitigation + Contingency→ Weekly review→ Escalation if breach
👤 Product Owner — 5 scripts

P4·PO·1 PO không buy-in pre-mortem ("tốn thời gian") PO does not buy-in pre-mortem ("time consuming") #

Pre-mortem ROIPre-mortem ROI👤 PO👤 PO⏱ 10 phút⏱ 10 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
PO: "Mình code đi, đừng mất 2h họp lo những cái chưa xảy ra".
PO: "Let's code, don't waste 2 hours of meetings worrying about things that haven't happened yet."
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Bỏ pre-mortem → tháng 3 hit risk đã có thể prevent → mất 2 tuần catch up.
Remove pre-mortem → hit risk can be prevented in March → takes 2 weeks to catch up.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh hiểu anh muốn move fast. Pre-mortem 2h = insurance cheap nhất mình mua được: Quy tắc: anh tập 8 người, hỏi 1 câu — "Giả sử 3 tháng nữa project fail. Lý do gì?". Mỗi người viết 5 lý do trong 10 phút. Cuộn lại → top 5 risk. Output: list 5 risk + owner + 1 mitigation/risk. Không phải plan dài 30 trang. Theo data Stanford: pre-mortem tăng ability identify reasons project fail thêm 30%. Đó là 30% issue mình prevent được. Nếu anh skip: mình ký off chấp nhận risk, ghi vào RAID. Sau này hit risk → không thể nói "ai mà ngờ".
I understand you want to move fast. Pre-mortem 2h =Cheapest insurance I can buy: Rules: 8 people practice, ask 1 question —"Let's say the project fails in 3 months. What's the reason?". Each person writes 5 reasons in 10 minutes. Scroll back → top 5 risks. Output:list 5 risks + owner + 1 mitigation/risk. Not a 30-page plan. According to Stanford data: pre-mortem increases the ability to identify reasons for project failure30%. That's 30% of the issues I can prevent. If you skip: I sign off, accept the risk, write it to RAID. Later, we hit risk → we can't say "who would have thought it".
💡 Lưu ý: Frame pre-mortem như insurance, không như "extra meeting". Cost low, payoff high.💡 Note: Frame pre-mortem is like insurance, not like "extra meeting". Cost low, payoff high.

P4·PO·2 PO báo "project không có risk" PO says "project has no risk" #

Explicit RAIDExplicit RAID👤 PO👤 PO⏱ 15 phút⏱ 15 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Status meeting: PO: "Project mình OK, không có risk gì". RAID log trống suốt project.
Meeting status: PO: "My project is OK, there are no risks." RAID log is empty throughout the project.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Đồng ý → tháng sau 3 risk bung ra cùng lúc → "ai mà ngờ".
Agree → next month 3 risks were released at the same time → "who would have thought".
✅ Script✅ Scripts
"Không có risk" là red flag mạnh nhất trong project management. Không phải project nào không có risk — chỉ là chưa nhìn ra. Đề xuất: 30 phút next, mình cùng đi qua 4 trục cố định: 1. Technical: legacy code, tech mới, scaling unknown. 2. People: ai key-person? Vacation? Attrition risk? 3. External: dependency từ team/vendor khác? Compliance change? 4. Business: market shift? Stakeholder thay đổi priority? Anh vào meeting với 1 list draft của anh (5–7 risk anh đã thấy). Mình review cùng nhau. Project có 0 risk = team naive. 5–10 risk + mitigation = team mature.
"No risk" isred flag strongest in project management. It's not that any project doesn't have risks — it's just that they haven't been seen yet. Suggestion: In the next 30 minutes, let's go over together4 fixed axes: 1. Technical: legacy code, new tech, scaling unknown. 2.People: who key-person? Vacation? Attrition risk? 3.External: dependency from another team/vendor? Compliance change? 4.Business: market shift? Stakeholder changes priority? He came into the meeting with his draft list (5–7 risks he saw). Let's review together. Project has 0 risk = team naive. 5–10 risks + mitigation = team maturity.
💡 Lưu ý: Force list bằng 4 trục — PO không thoát "không có gì". Tự draft trước → giảm cognitive load PO.💡 Note: Force list with 4 axes — PO does not exit "nothing". Draft yourself first → reduce cognitive load PO.

P4·PO·3 PO escalate trực tiếp lên VP, skip cấp PO escalates directly to VP, skips level #

Escalation LadderEscalation Ladder👤 PO👤 PO⏱ 15 phút⏱ 15 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Issue xảy ra hôm qua, PO email thẳng VP. Director của EM mới biết qua VP forward.
Issue happened yesterday, PO emailed VP directly. EM's Director just found out through VP forward.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Bỏ qua → tạo precedent skip cấp → director mất visibility, EM mất trust.
Ignore → create precedent skip level → director loses visibility, EM loses trust.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh muốn nói thẳng về vụ escalate VP hôm qua — không phải để complain, mà để align process. Khi PO skip cấp: • Director của anh mất context → trả lời sai/chậm cho VP. • Anh không có cơ hội xử trước khi escalate → trông như anh không control. • VP gặp 1 vấn đề mà 3 cấp dưới chưa bàn → quyết định vội. Escalation ladder mình thống nhất: • Lvl 1 (issue thường): anh + PO, < 24h. • Lvl 2 (cần resource): + Director, < 48h. • Lvl 3 (cần priority shift): + VP, < 1 tuần. Trừ P0 production incident ảnh hưởng customer — lúc đó anh chủ động ping VP cùng anh. Lần sau, ping anh trước. Anh phản hồi trong <2h, không bottleneck.
I want to speak frankly about the VP escalation yesterday — not to complain, but to align the process. When PO skip level: • Your Director lost context → wrong/slow response to VP. • You didn't have a chance to act before it escalated → it looked like you had no control. • VP encountered a problem that 3 subordinates had not discussed yet → made a hasty decision.Escalation ladder I agree: •Lvl 1(regular issue): you + PO, < 24 hours. •Lvl 2(need resources): + Director, < 48h. •Lvl 3(need priority shift): + VP, < 1 week. Apart fromP0 production incident influence the customer — at that time he proactively pinged the VP with him. Next time, ping me first. He responded in <2 hours, no bottleneck.
💡 Lưu ý: Skip cấp = trust issue 2 chiều. Đối thoại thẳng + đặt expectation rõ.💡 Note: Skip grant = 2-way trust issue. Direct dialogue + set clear expectations.

P4·PO·4 PO yêu cầu "hide bad news" với stakeholder PO requests to "hide bad news" from stakeholders #

Transparency principleTransparency principle👤 PO👤 PO⏱ 20 phút⏱ 20 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Project trễ 2 sprint. PO: "Đừng nói VP, mình catch up trong tuần".
Project is 2 sprints late. PO: "Don't say VP, I'll catch up within the week."
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Hide → "tuần catch up" không xảy ra → tháng sau VP biết, mất trust gấp đôi.
Hide → "catch up week" doesn't happen → next month VP knows, doubles loss of trust.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh không thể giấu bad news với stakeholder, dù muốn giúp anh. Lý do thẳng: 1. Stakeholder ghét bất ngờ hơn ghét tin xấu — biết sớm = chuẩn bị, biết muộn = sốc + giảm trust. 2. Nếu mình "catch up không kịp", VP biết tháng sau → 2 lần fail (project + cover-up). 3. Anh và anh đều mất credibility — nó stick lâu hơn 1 sprint trễ. Đề xuất alternative: • Status thành Amber tuần này, không Green. • Email VP với BLUF: "Project trễ 2 sprint do X. Plan recovery: Y. Yêu cầu: Z." • Anh + anh cùng vào meeting với VP, defend plan. Bad news + plan + ask = professionalism. Bad news muộn + spin = mất sự nghiệp.
You can't hide bad news from stakeholders, even if you want to help you. Straight reason: 1.Stakeholders hate surprises more than they hate bad news— knowing early = preparation, knowing late = shock + reduced trust. 2. If I "can't catch up in time", VP knows next month → 2 failures (project + cover-up). 3. You both lose credibility — it sticks longer than a late sprint.Suggested alternative: • Status completedAmber This week, no Green. • Email VP with BLUF:"Project is 2 sprints late due to X. Plan recovery: Y. Requirements: Z."• You + you go to the meeting with VP, defend plan. Bad news + plan + ask = professionalism. Late bad news + spin = lost career.
💡 Lưu ý: Đứng vững trên transparency. Đây là career-protecting cho cả EM và PO, không phải "rigid".💡 Note: Stand firmly on transparency. This is career-protecting for both EM and PO, not "rigid".

P4·PO·5 PO không follow up mitigation, đợi issue rồi mới react PO does not follow up mitigation, waits for issue before reacting #

Weekly RAID reviewWeekly RAID review👤 PO👤 PO⏱ 30 phút/tuần⏱ 30 minutes/week
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
RAID log có 6 risk identified, không ai update mitigation 4 tuần. 2 risk thành issue.
RAID log has 6 identified risks, no one has updated mitigations for 4 weeks. 2 risks become issues.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
RAID log đẻ ra rồi để đó → vô nghĩa hơn không có.
RAID log is created and left there → more meaningless than not having it.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
RAID log không update = nguy hiểm hơn không có RAID. Mình bỏ thời gian identify, không xử = waste. Cadence mới: • Weekly RAID review 30 phút, Friday 3pm. • Mỗi risk có owner (R) + accountable (A) + deadline mitigation. • Mỗi tuần update trạng thái: Open / In Progress / Mitigated / Triggered (became issue). • Risk score (P × I) re-assess: tăng/giảm/ổn định. Cụ thể 2 risk vừa thành issue: 1. Anh document blameless postmortem: vì sao mình spot risk nhưng không mitigate? 2. Action items: ai làm, khi nào. 3. Feed back vào pre-mortem template để tránh lặp. Risk-management-by-spreadsheet không work. Phải có nhịp tuần và human accountability.
RAID log not updated = more dangerous than no RAID. I spent time identifying, not processing = waste. New Cadence: •Weekly RAID review30 minutes, Friday 3pm. • Every risk hasowner(R) +accountable(A) +deadline mitigation. • Update status every week:Open / In Progress / Mitigated / Triggered (became issue). • Risk score (P × I) re-assess: increase/decrease/stable. Specifically, 2 risks have just become issues: 1. Anh document blameless postmortem: why do we spot risk but not mitigate it? 2. Action items: who does it, when. 3. Feed back into pre-mortem template to avoid repetition. Risk-management-by-spreadsheet does not work. There must be discipline and human accountability.
💡 Lưu ý: Setup recurring 30p/tuần. Skip 1 lần = bắt đầu chết slow. Discipline + visibility.💡 Note: Setup recurring 30 minutes/week. Skip 1 time = start to die slowly. Discipline + visibility.
📐 Business Analyst — 5 scripts

P4·BA·1 BA treat assumption như fact BA treat assumption as fact #

Assumption trackingAssumption tracking📐 BA📐 BA⏱ 15 phút⏱ 15 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
BA viết spec dựa trên "user sẽ luôn online" — production user offline 30% time → feature broken.
BA wrote the spec based on "user will always be online" — production user offline 30% of the time → feature broken.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Spec không nói rõ assumption → dev không challenge → ship → fail.
Spec does not clearly state assumption → dev does not challenge → ship → fail.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Assumption ngầm = risk lớn nhất ẩn trong spec. Em phải list explicit: Trong template story add ô "Assumptions": • User behavior: "Giả định user luôn online" • Data: "Giả định response < 1MB" • Tech: "Giả định API X stable" • Business: "Giả định pricing không thay đổi 3 tháng" Mỗi assumption phải có: • Validation status: confirmed / unverified / risky • If wrong: hậu quả gì? (1 dòng) • Owner: ai validate? Trong refinement, dev có quyền challenge assumption. "Giả định user online" → có data nào support? Nếu không → đây là risk, ghi vào RAID. Không có spec không có assumption — chỉ có spec không list ra.
Implicit assumption = largest risk hidden in the spec. I have tolist explicit: In story template add cell"Assumptions": • User behavior: "Assume the user is always online" • Data: "Assume response < 1MB" • Tech: "Assume API X stable" • Business: "Assume pricing does not change for 3 months" Each assumption must have: •Validation status: confirmed / unverified / risky •If wrong: what are the consequences? (1 line) •Owner: who validates? In refinement, developers have the right to challenge assumptions. "Assume user is online" → is there any data to support? If not → this is a risk, write to RAID. No specs, no assumptions — just specs without a list.
💡 Lưu ý: "A" trong RAID = Assumption. Update đều như Risk. Assumption sai = root cause của nhiều issue.💡 Note:"A" in RAID = Assumption. Updates are the same as Risk. Wrong assumption = root cause of many issues.

P4·BA·2 BA nói "có risk" nhưng không quantify BA said "there is risk" but no investigation #

Risk Matrix (P × I)Risk Matrix (P × I)📐 BA📐 BA⏱ 10 phút⏱ 10 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
BA: "Cái này risky lắm" — không nói risky như nào, không action.
BA: "This is very risky" — don't say how risky, no action.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Vague "risky" → mọi thứ đều risky → không thứ tự ưu tiên → không xử.
Vague "risky" → everything is risky → no priority → no treatment.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
"Risky" không actionable. Em quantify thành 2 con số: Probability (1–5): khả năng xảy ra trong project window. • 1 = rất ít (<10%) · 5 = chắc xảy ra (>80%) Impact (1–5): nếu xảy ra, ảnh hưởng? • 1 = inconvenience · 5 = project fail / customer churn Score = P × I: • 1–4 (low): note, không action. • 5–11 (medium): owner + monitor weekly. • 12–25 (high): mitigation plan + contingency, weekly review. Cụ thể "vendor có thể trễ": • P = 4 (vendor đã trễ 2 lần trước) • I = 5 (block toàn epic) • Score = 20 → high → cần plan B. Sau workshop 30p, em sẽ có 8–12 risk có score → biết cái nào đáng dồn time, cái nào để vậy.
"Risky" is not actionable. I calculated into 2 numbers:Probability (1–5): possibility in project window. • 1 = very unlikely (<10%) · 5 = likely (>80%)Impact (1–5): if happens, impact? • 1 = inconvenience · 5 = project failure / customer churn Score = P × I: • 1–4 (low): note, no action. • 5–11 (medium): owner + monitor weekly. • 12–25 (high): mitigation plan + contingency, weekly review. Specifically "vendor may be late": • P = 4 (vendor was late 2 times before) • I = 5 (all epic block) • Score = 20 → high → need plan B. After the 30-minute workshop, you will have 8–12 risks with scores → know which ones are worth spending time on, and which ones should be left alone.
💡 Lưu ý: Force numeric. Lo lắng định tính = không có gì. P × I = ngôn ngữ chung với PO/exec.💡 Note: Force numeric. Qualitative anxiety = nothing. P × I = common language with PO/exec.

P4·BA·3 BA mới phát hiện dependency 3rd party BA just discovered the 3rd party dependency #

RAID + MitigationRAID + Mitigation📐 BA📐 BA⏱ 15 phút⏱ 15 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Mid-project, BA nhận ra feature phải tích hợp 3rd party có legal contract chưa ký.
Mid-project, BA realized the feature had to integrate a 3rd party with an unsigned legal contract.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Im lặng đợi legal "khi nào xong" → 4 tuần sau vẫn chưa xong → epic block.
Silently waiting for legal "when will it be finished" → 4 weeks later still not finished → epic block.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Cảm ơn em raise. Đây là dependency risk đặc biệt nguy hiểm vì 3rd party + legal — ngoài tầm kiểm soát team. Bước nhanh: 1. Update RAID log: - Dependency: 3rd party API + legal contract - Risk: nếu không sign trong 4 tuần, epic block - P = 3, I = 5, Score = 15 → high 2. Mitigation: anh + em + Legal họp tuần này, có deadline rõ. 3. Contingency (nếu sign trễ): - Build với mock API trước → giảm 30% risk - Có thể release feature subset không cần 3rd party 4. Weekly status: Legal owner update → anh report PO. Nguyên tắc: dependency ngoài team = phải có plan B lúc identify, không phải lúc hit.
Thank you raise. This isdependency risk Especially dangerous because 3rd party + legal — beyond the team's control. Quick steps: 1. Update RAID log: -Dependency: 3rd party API + legal contract -Risk: if you don't sign in 4 weeks, epic block - P = 3, I = 5, Score = 15 → high 2.Mitigation: me + me + Legal meeting this week, with a clear deadline. 3.Contingency(if signing late): - Build with mock API first → reduce risk by 30%. - Feature subset can be released without a 3rd party 4.Weekly status: Legal owner update → you report PO. Principle: dependency outside the team = must haveplan Bidentify, not hit.
💡 Lưu ý: Identify ≠ done. Phải có owner + mitigation + contingency. 3rd party luôn cần plan B.💡 Note: Identify ≠ done. Must have owner + mitigation + contingency. The 3rd party always needs a plan B.

P4·BA·4 BA postpone update RAID "khi có thời gian" BA postpones RAID update "when there is time" #

Weekly cadenceWeekly cadence📐 BA📐 BA⏱ 5 phút⏱ 5 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
BA: "Em busy cập nhật RAID khi có thời gian". 3 tuần không update.
BA: "I'm busy updating RAID when I have time." 3 weeks no update.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Để BA tự quản → RAID stale → giá trị = 0.
Let BA self-manage → RAID stale → value = 0.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
RAID không phải "khi có thời gian" — nó là lifeblood của project. Anh đặt cadence cố định: • Mỗi Wednesday standup: 5 phút, em scan RAID và point ra 1 thay đổi trong tuần. • Mỗi Friday 3pm: 30 phút full RAID review (anh + em + tech lead + PO). • Em là RAID owner — không có nghĩa em viết hết, có nghĩa em make sure log up-to-date. Cụ thể: • Risk score thay đổi không? P tăng/giảm? • Mitigation action có happen? Owner nào trễ? • Issue mới xuất hiện? Assumption nào prove sai? 5 phút Wed + 30p Fri = 35p/tuần. So với cost 1 issue chưa mitigate explode → ROI cực cao. Anh sẽ check RAID mỗi Friday — không update = đỏ trong 1-1.
RAID isn't "when there's time" — it isproject's lifeblood. You set the cadence fixed: •Every Wednesday standup: 5 minutes, I scan the RAID and point it out1 changeduring the week. •Every Friday 3pm: 30 minutes full RAID review (you + me + tech lead + PO). • I'm a RAID owner — it doesn't mean I write everything, it means I make sure log up-to-date. Specifically: • Has the risk score changed? P increase/decrease? • Does mitigation action happen? Which owner is late? • New issue appears? Which assumption proves wrong? 5 minutes Wed + 30 minutes Fri = 35 minutes/week. Compared to cost 1 issue that has not been mitigated explode → extremely high ROI. I will check RAID every Friday — no updates = red in January 1.
💡 Lưu ý: Cadence + accountability. RAID không cập nhật là RAID không tồn tại.💡 Note: Cadence + accountability. RAID that is not updated is RAID that does not exist.

P4·BA·5 BA panic vì issue lớn ở UAT BA panic because of big issue at UAT #

Issue TriageIssue Triage📐 BA📐 BA⏱ 20 phút⏱ 20 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
UAT 1 ngày trước launch, BA phát hiện flow X không work cho user segment Y. Panic ping toàn team.
UAT 1 day before launch, BA discovered that flow X was not working for user segment Y. Panic pinged the entire team.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Cả team drop hết → fix vội → nửa đêm → bug phụ phát sinh → fail launch.
The whole team dropped everything → quickly fixed it → midnight → a secondary bug arose → failed launch.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh hiểu panic — nhưng panic + cả team xông vào = chaos, không = fix nhanh hơn. Triage protocol — 20 phút, anh chủ trì: 1. Reproduce + scope: bug ảnh hưởng segment Y. % user trong segment? Severity? 2. Triage decision (3 lựa chọn): - Block launch: nếu > 20% user impact + no workaround. - Launch + hotfix: nếu < 5% impact, có workaround, fix < 24h. - Launch + known issue: nếu rare edge case, fix sau. 3. Communication: 1 BLUF email cho stakeholder, không 5 messages panic. 4. Single point of contact: anh + 1 dev fix, team khác continue plan launch. Anh quyết trong 20 phút với data, không quyết trong 5 phút với cảm xúc.
I understand panic — but panic + the whole team rushing in = chaos, no = faster fix. Triage protocol — 20 minutes, he presides: 1.Reproduce + scope: bug affects segment Y. % users in segment? Severity? 2.Triage decision(3 options): -Block launch: if > 20% user impact + no workaround. -Launch + hotfix: if < 5% impact, there is a workaround, fix < 24 hours. -Launch + known issue: if rare edge case, fix later. 3.Communication: 1 BLUF email to stakeholders, no 5 panic messages. 4.Single point of contact: he + 1 dev fix, other team continue plan launch. You decide in 20 minutes with data, not in 5 minutes with emotions.
💡 Lưu ý: EM = anchor calm trong issue. Có protocol từ trước → không chế ad-hoc khi panic.💡 Note: EM = anchor calm in issue. Have a pre-existing protocol → do not control ad-hoc when panicking.
💻 Developer — 5 scripts

P4·DEV·1 Dev thấy tech risk nhưng không raise (sợ overreact) Dev sees tech risk but doesn't raise (fear of overreact) #

Tech risk forumTech risk forum💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 15 phút⏱ 15 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Dev biết database schema có vấn đề scaling, nhưng "đợi lúc cần thiết hãy nói".
Dev knows the database schema has scaling problems, but "wait until it's necessary to say it".
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Đợi đến khi production scale fail mới fix → 10× cost.
Wait until production scale fails to fix → 10× cost.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh quan sát em hold technical concerns. Cho anh biết em đang lo gì — không phải để act ngay, là để document. Anh tạo tech risk forum 30 phút mỗi 2 tuần, riêng dev: • Mỗi dev raise 1 risk technical họ thấy (db, performance, security, dependency). • Không phải họp "fix ngay". Output: ghi vào RAID, có owner + plan. • Risk có thể "monitor" mà không action — nhưng visible. Cụ thể schema concern em vừa nói: • Anh add vào RAID: "DB schema scaling — P=3, I=4, score=12 → high" • Owner: em. Action: 1 spike 2 ngày để model load & ra recommendation. • PO biết: nếu hit, cần budget refactor 1 sprint trong 6 tháng tới. Raise risk = trách nhiệm chuyên môn. Im lặng = đẩy cost cho team sau.
I observe that you hold technical concerns. Tell me what you're worried about — it's not to act right away, it's todocument. document. He createdtech risk forum30 minutes every 2 weeks, dev only: • Each dev raises a technical risk they see (db, performance, security, dependency). • Not a "fix it now" meeting. Output: write to RAID, have owner + plan. • Risk can be "monitored" without action — but visible. Specifically, the schema concern you just said: • You add to RAID:"DB schema scaling — P=3, I=4, score=12 → high"• Owner: me. Action: 1 spike 2 days for model to load & make recommendation. • PO knows: if it's a hit, it needs a budget refactor for 1 sprint in the next 6 months. Raise risk = professional responsibility. Silence = pushing costs to the next team.
💡 Lưu ý: Tạo channel an toàn cho dev raise tech risk — không bị "PO panic" ngay. Document > act-now.💡 Note: Create a safe channel for devs to raise tech risk — don't get "PO panic" right away. Document > act-now.

P4·DEV·2 Dev fix bug production không document Dev fixes production bugs without documentation #

Blameless PostmortemBlameless Postmortem💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 1h sau incident⏱ 1 hour after the incident
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Dev fix P1 bug 2 giờ sáng, không doc gì. Tuần sau bug tương tự xảy ra ở module khác — không ai có context.
Dev fixed P1 bug at 2 am, didn't read anything. The following week the same bug occurred in another module — no one had context.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Vui vì "fix nhanh" → không học → bug repeat → dev đó về sau leave → kiến thức biến mất.
Happy because "quick fix" → no learning → bug repeat → that dev later leaves → knowledge disappears.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Cảm ơn em đã save day. Nhưng fix mà không document = mình mất 50% giá trị của incident đó. Trong 24h sau mọi P0/P1, mình làm blameless postmortem 1 trang: 1. Timeline: phát hiện → diagnose → fix → recovery (timestamps cụ thể). 2. Root cause: dùng 5 Whys. Không dừng ở "code bug" — đào đến process/system level. 3. What went well: alert trigger, recovery, communication. 4. What went wrong: gì làm chậm hoặc khó. 5. Action items: 3–5 thứ. Mỗi cái có owner + deadline. Vào sprint sau. Quy tắc blameless: không có "ai sai". Có "system cho phép sai". Mục tiêu: cùng bug không repeat lần 2. Em viết draft trong giờ làm, anh review. Mình share với team trong sprint review.
Thank you for saving the day. But fixing without documentation = I lose 50% of the value of that incident. Within 24 hours after every P0/P1, I do itblameless postmortem1 page: 1.Timeline: detect → diagnose → fix → recovery (specific timestamps). 2.Root cause: use5 Whys. Don't stop at "code bugs" — dig to the process/system level. 3.What went well: alert trigger, recovery, communication. 4.What went wrong: something that is slow or difficult. 5.Action items: 3–5 things. Each has an owner + deadline. Go to the next sprint. Rulesblameless: there is no "one who is wrong". There is a "wrong permission system". Goal: don't repeat the same bug a second time. I write a draft during work hours, you review it. I share with the team during sprint review.
💡 Lưu ý: Postmortem trong 24h, không 2 tuần sau. Memory mất nhanh. Blameless = chìa khóa để dev viết thật.💡 Note: Postmortem within 24 hours, not 2 weeks later. Memory disappears quickly. Blameless = the key to real dev writing.

P4·DEV·3 Dev shortcut bypass test "vì gấp" Dev shortcut bypass test "because it's urgent" #

Quality gateQuality gate💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 10 phút⏱ 10 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Dev: "Em skip unit test cho story này, gấp lắm. Sẽ add sau".
Dev: "I skipped unit testing for this story, it's urgent. Will add later."
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Đồng ý "lần này thôi" → "lần này" thành 100% → tech debt + bug rate tăng.
Agree "just this time" → "this time" becomes 100% → tech debt + bug rate increases.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh hiểu pressure. Nhưng "test sau" = 95% không bao giờ add. Anh nói thẳng: 3 lựa chọn: 1. Test ít hơn nhưng đúng critical path: 80% coverage giảm xuống 50%, focus happy path + 2 edge case quan trọng nhất. Vẫn pass DoD. 2. Cắt scope story: bỏ 1 sub-feature, có time test đầy đủ. 3. Trễ 1 ngày: anh đi nói PO trade-off "1 ngày trễ vs zero test coverage trên feature payment". Cái không được phép: ship code không test, hứa "add sau". Đó không phải shortcut — đó là tech debt invisible. Em chọn 1 trong 3?
I understand pressure. But "test later" = 95% never add. He said frankly:3 options: 1. Test less but correct critical path: 80% coverage reduced to 50%, focus happy path + 2 most important edge cases. Still pass DoD. 2.Cut story scope: remove 1 sub-feature, have full testing time. 3.1 day late: you go and say PO trade-off "1 day late vs zero test coverage on feature payment". Femaledoes not Allowed: ship code without testing, promise to "add later". That's not a shortcut — that's tech debt invisible. Should I choose 1 out of 3?
💡 Lưu ý: Bring 3 alternative thay vì say-no. Force trade-off conscious, không silent.💡 Note: Bring 3 alternative instead of say-no. Force trade-off consciously, not silently.

P4·DEV·4 Tech debt pile up không đưa vào RAID Tech debt pile up is not included in RAID #

Debt visibilityDebt visibility💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 30 phút⏱ 30 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Team biết 8 chỗ tech debt nguy hiểm, không tracked. PO không bao giờ allocate time fix.
The team knows 8 areas where tech debt is dangerous, not tracked. PO never allocates time fix.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Coi tech debt là "việc dev tự xử" → không trong scope → never happen → 1 ngày explode.
Consider tech debt as "dev's own business" → not in scope → never happen → explode one day.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Tech debt invisible với PO = nó không tồn tại trong universe của họ. Mình phải visible hóa: Mỗi tech debt → 1 ticket trong RAID/backlog với: • Cái gì broken: 1 dòng, không jargon. • Risk nếu không fix: incident? Slow dev? Hard onboard? Security? • P × I score: same matrix với risk khác. • Cost fix: estimate (story points). • Cost không fix: 6 tháng tới = bao nhiêu giờ dev waste? Bao nhiêu bug khả năng? Workshop 30p với team: list 8 tech debt, score, sort. PO meeting hàng quý: mình propose 20% capacity dành cho top tech debt. Show tradeoff bằng số: "Skip refactor X = 6 tháng tới mất ~30 giờ dev/tháng debug = 1 sprint waste/quarter". Tech debt không có sales pitch = bị defer mãi.
Tech debt invisible with PO = it does not exist in their universe. I mustvisible: Each tech debt → 1 ticket in RAID/backlog with: •What's broken?: 1 line, no jargon. •Risk if not fixed: incident? Slow dev? Hard onboard? Security? •P × I score: same matrix with different risk. •Cost fixed: estimate (story points). •Cost is not fixed: Next 6 months = how many dev hours wasted? How many bugs are possible? 30 minute workshop with team: list 8 tech debt, score, sort. PO meeting quarterly: I propose20% capacity for top tech debt. Show tradeoff in numbers:"Skip refactor X = next 6 months ~30 dev hours/month debugging = 1 sprint waste/quarter". Tech debt without a sales pitch = forever deferred.
💡 Lưu ý: Tech debt = sales game với PO. Phải có data + cost both sides.💡 Note: Tech debt = game sales with PO. Must have data + cost both sides.

P4·DEV·5 Dev panic khi P0 production Dev panics when P0 production #

Incident Calm ProtocolIncident Calm Protocol💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 10 phút⏱ 10 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Service down 30 phút. Dev primary on-call panic, đẩy 5 commit fix nhanh không test, làm tệ hơn.
Service down 30 minutes. Dev primary on-call panic, pushed 5 quick fix commits without testing, made worse.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Để dev solo handle → fatigue + panic → bug nhân lên.
Let the dev handle it solo → fatigue + panic → bugs multiply.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh đã ở đây. Slow down 30 giây. P0 protocol (anh dẫn dắt): 1. Stop the bleed: revert/rollback trước, fix sau. Restore service, không fix root cause ngay. 2. Roles: - Incident commander (anh): coordinate, communicate. - Tech lead: diagnose + fix. - Comms: BA hoặc PM update stakeholder mỗi 15 phút. - Em (primary on-call): execute, không phải decide alone. 3. 1 channel only: #incident-{id}. Mọi comm ở đó. Slack DM = lost context. 4. Timeline log: Slack pinned message, update mỗi action. 5. Sau khi recover: ngủ trước. Postmortem 24h sau, không ngay đêm. Em không cần solve alone. Anh đứng đây cho đến khi green.
I was here. Slow down 30 seconds.P0 protocol(he leads): 1.Stop the bleeding: revert/rollback first, fix later. Restore service, do not fix root cause immediately. 2.Roles: - Incident commander(eng): coordinate, communicate. -Tech leads: diagnose + fix. -Comms: BA or PM updates stakeholders every 15 minutes. - Em (primary on-call): execute, not decide alone. 3.1 channel only: #incident-{id}. Every comm there. Slack DM = lost context. 4.Timeline log: Slack pinned message, updates each action. 5.After recovery: sleep first. Postmortem 24 hours later, not overnight. I don't need to solve alone. I stand here until the green.
💡 Lưu ý: EM = incident commander mặc định. Dev focus fix, không multitask comm. Có protocol viết sẵn từ peacetime.💡 Note: EM = default incident commander. Dev focus fix, no multitask comm. There is a pre-written protocol from peacetime.

📣 P5 — Stakeholder Communication (15)

Status update minh bạch, expectation alignment, RACI rõ ràng, biết nói "không" có lý. Bài học: stakeholder ghét bất ngờ hơn ghét tin xấu.

🎯 Kỹ thuật chủ đạo

🚦

RAG Status (Red/Amber/Green)

Mỗi tuần 1 màu cho project. Đỏ phải kèm cause + ask. Xanh không bao giờ tự nhiên thành đỏ — luôn qua amber.

Dùng khi: weekly update
📊

RACI Matrix

Responsible · Accountable · Consulted · Informed. 1 task chỉ có 1 A. Hỏi "ai là A?" là câu mạnh nhất.

Dùng khi: kickoff cross-team
🎯

BLUF (Bottom Line Up Front)

Câu đầu = kết luận. Sau đó mới context. Email/slack/standup đều dùng. Tiết kiệm thời gian executive.

Dùng khi: report up
🛑

Say-No có lý (Yes-No-Yes)

Yes với giá trị họ care · No với yêu cầu hiện tại · Yes với cách khác. Không bao giờ "no" trống.

Dùng khi: scope creep
📐

Stakeholder Map (P/I)

Power × Interest. High-power high-interest = manage closely. Đừng đối xử mọi stakeholder như nhau.

Dùng khi: kickoff
📝

Email recap 24h

Sau mọi cuộc họp/quyết định: gửi email recap trong 24h. "Im lặng = đồng ý". Tránh he-said-she-said.

Dùng khi: sau decision
Stakeholder map→ RACI→ Weekly RAG→ BLUF email→ Say-no khi cần→ Recap 24h
👤 Product Owner — 5 scripts

P5·PO·1 PO ghost stakeholder, không weekly update PO ghost stakeholders, no weekly updates #

Weekly RAG cadenceWeekly RAG cadence👤 PO👤 PO⏱ 30 phút setup⏱ 30 minutes setup
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
4 tuần PO không update stakeholder. Stakeholder tự nghi → email DM panic.
4 weeks PO does not update stakeholders. Stakeholder doubts himself → email DM panic.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Đợi stakeholder hỏi mới reply → reactive → mất control narrative.
Wait for the stakeholder to ask before responding → react → lose narrative control.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Stakeholder ghét silence hơn ghét bad news. Mình setup weekly RAG update — 5 phút viết, không 30: Template (Friday 4pm): ``` Project: [name] Status: 🟢 Green / 🟡 Amber / 🔴 Red 📍 Done this week (3 bullets max) 🎯 Next week (3 bullets max) ⚠️ Blockers / risks (1–3, có ask cụ thể) 📊 Burn-up: [X% complete, Y sprint left] ``` Quy tắc: • Status thay đổi (Green→Amber, etc.) phải có cause + ask. • 🔴 Red không bao giờ xuất hiện đột ngột — luôn qua 🟡 trước. • Send chính xác Friday 4pm. Stakeholder build expectation. Anh sẽ draft cho anh tuần đầu, anh review trong 5 phút và send. Sau 4 tuần, anh tự làm.
Stakeholders hate silence more than they hate bad news. I set upweekly RAG update— 5 minutes of writing, not 30:Template (Friday 4pm): ``` Project: [name] Status: 🟢 Green / 🟡 Amber / 🔴 Red 📍 Done this week (3 bullets max) 🎯 Next week (3 bullets max) ⚠️ Blockers / risks (1–3, with specific ask) 📊 Burn-up: [X% complete, Y sprint left] ``` Rules: • Status change (Green→Amber, etc.) must be presentcause + ask. • 🔴 Red never appears suddenly — always passes 🟡 first. • Send exactly Friday 4pm. Stakeholder builds expectations. I'll draft it for you the first week, I'll review it in 5 minutes and send it. After 4 weeks, he did it himself.
💡 Lưu ý: Nhịp đều > chất lượng cao 1 lần. Send đúng giờ để stakeholder không "tự sáng tạo" version trong đầu họ.💡 Note: Regular rhythm > high quality once. Send on time so stakeholders don't "invent" versions in their heads.

P5·PO·2 PO over-promise CEO ngoài team capacity PO over-promise CEO in addition to team capacity #

Transparent push-backTransparent push-back👤 PO👤 PO⏱ 20 phút⏱ 20 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
PO họp với CEO → commit deliverable Q4 ngoài capacity team. Về team báo "phải làm".
PO meeting with CEO → commit deliverable Q4 beyond team capacity. About the "must do" newspaper team.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Im lặng nhận → team OT/burnout/miss → CEO mất trust khi miss → PO blame team.
Accept silently → OT team/burnout/miss → CEO loses trust when missing → PO blames team.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh hiểu áp lực CEO. Nhưng commit ngoài capacity = setup mình thua. Anh phải bring data: Anh đi làm capacity model 1 trang trong 1h: ``` Q4 capacity: ~280 pts (7 sprint × 40 pts velocity) Đã commit: - Project A: 120 pts ✓ - Bug + maintenance: 60 pts (15% buffer thường) - KTLO + on-call: 40 pts ──────── Available cho mới: 60 pts CEO ask: 180 pts → gap 120 pts (200% over) ``` Anh và anh setup 1 follow-up với CEO trong 48h, present: • Option 1: cắt scope project mới xuống 60 pts. • Option 2: defer project A 1 sprint, làm song song. • Option 3: thêm headcount (12 tuần ramp). CEO không phản đối số đúng. Họ phản đối "không chắc". Mình có data → CEO sẽ cùng quyết — anh vẫn hero.
He understands CEO pressure. But committing outside capacity = setup I lose. You must bring data: I go to workcapacity model1 page in 1 hour: ``` Q4 capacity: ~280 pts (7 sprint × 40 pts velocity) Committed: - Project A: 120 pts ✓ - Bug + maintenance: 60 pts (15% normal buffer) - KTLO + on-call: 40 pts ──────── Available for new: 60 pts CEO ask: 180 pts → gap 120 pts (200% over) ``` You and I set up a follow-up with the CEO within 48 hours, present: • Option 1: cut the new project scope to 60 pts. • Option 2: defer project A 1 sprint, done in parallel. • Option 3: add headcount (12 week ramp). The CEO did not object to the correct number. They protested "unlikely". We have the data → The CEO will decide together — you're still a hero.
💡 Lưu ý: Help PO walk back commitment sai bằng data + alternative. Đừng để PO tự xử → đẩy team OT.💡 Note: Help PO walk back commitment wrong with data + alternative. Don't let the PO handle it himself → push the OT team.

P5·PO·3 PO không có RACI cho cross-team project PO does not have RACI for cross-team projects #

RACI MatrixRACI Matrix👤 PO👤 PO⏱ 1h workshop⏱ 1 hour workshop
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Project có 4 team. Decision lặp đi lặp lại, ai cũng giả định "team kia quyết". Block 2 tuần.
The project has 4 teams. Decisions are repeated over and over, everyone assumes "the other team decides". Block 2 weeks.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Không có RACI → mặc định ai to tiếng nhất quyết → quyết sai → đổ lỗi qua lại.
There is no RACI → by default, whoever insists loudly → decides wrongly → blame goes back and forth.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Mình mất 2 tuần vì không rõ ai có quyền decide. Đề xuất 1h RACI workshop với tất cả team lead: Format: bảng — mỗi cột là 1 quyết định loại; mỗi hàng là 1 role. Decision categories: • Scope & priority • Tech architecture • API contract • Release timing • User-facing UX • Security/compliance Mỗi cell có duy nhất 1 chữ: • Responsible (làm việc, có thể nhiều người) • Accountable (decision owner, chỉ 1 người) • Consulted (cần feedback trước decide) • Informed (cần biết sau decide) Quy tắc: 1 cell = 1 A. Nếu 2 người claim A → escalate ngay. Sau workshop: 1 sheet treo ở Slack channel. Mọi disagreement, hỏi: "ai là A?" — câu hỏi mạnh nhất trong cross-team project.
It took me 2 weeks because it wasn't clear who had the right to decide. Recommended 1 hourRACI workshop with all team leads:Format: table — each column is 1 type decision; Each row is 1 role. Decision categories: • Scope & priority • Tech architecture • API contracts • Release timing • User-facing UX • Security/compliance Each cell has only 1 letter: •R esponsible (work, can be done by many people) •A ccountable (decision owner,just 1 person) •C onsulted (need feedback before deciding) •I nformed (need to know after deciding)Rules: 1 cell = 1 A. If 2 people claim A → escalate immediately. After the workshop: 1 sheet hangs in the Slack channel. Every dissent, ask: "who is A?" — the strongest question in a cross-team project.
💡 Lưu ý: RACI ngầm = RACI sai. Bắt buộc viết ra. 1 A duy nhất / cell.💡 Note: Implicit RACI = False RACI. Required to write down. 1 A single/cell.

P5·PO·4 PO say-yes mọi yêu cầu, không filter PO say-yes to all requests, no filtering #

Yes-No-YesYes-No-Yes👤 PO👤 PO⏱ 15 phút⏱ 15 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
PO nhận yêu cầu từ Sales, CS, Marketing — đều "yes" → backlog 200 ticket → team confused về priority.
PO receives requests from Sales, CS, Marketing — all "yes" → backlog 200 tickets → team confused about priority.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
"Yes" trống → tạo expectation → khi không deliver → mất trust với 5 nhóm cùng lúc.
"Yes" is empty → creates expectation → when not delivered → loses trust with 5 groups at the same time.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
"Yes" là PO tự bảo vệ trong moment, nhưng phá long-term. Anh dạy framework Yes-No-Yes: Yes với giá trị họ care (acknowledge): "Em hiểu Sales cần feature X để win deal Z — đó là quan trọng cho công ty." No với yêu cầu hiện tại (boundary có lý): "Hiện Q4 backlog đã commit 100% capacity cho project A. Nếu mình thêm X bây giờ, project A trễ 3 tuần — và nó cũng quan trọng cho retention." Yes với cách khác (path forward): "Anh có 2 options: 1. Mình add X vào Q1 backlog — anh sẽ ưu tiên trong refinement đầu Q1. 2. Nếu Sales urgent: mình bring vào prioritization meeting tuần sau với data deal Z, decide trade-off với project A. Anh chọn 1 nhé?" Không bao giờ "no" trống. Không bao giờ "yes" trống. Always: yes-no-yes.
"Yes" is a PO that protects itself in the moment, but breaks the long-term. He teaches frameworksYes-No-Yes: Yes withvalue they care(acknowledge): "I understand Sales needs feature X to win deal Z — that's important for the company."No withcurrent request(boundary makes sense): "Currently the Q4 backlog has committed 100% capacity to project A. If I add X now, project A is 3 weeks late — and it's also important for retention."Yes withother(path forward): "You have 2 options: 1. I add X to the Q1 backlog — I will give priority in refining at the beginning of Q1. 2. If Sales are urgent: I bring it to the prioritization meeting next week with data deal Z, decide trade-off with project A. Can you choose 1?" Never "full" empty. "yes" is never empty. Always: yes-no-yes.
💡 Lưu ý: Saying-yes không phải nice — saying-no có lý mới respectful. Stakeholder appreciate hơn.💡 Note: Saying-yes is not nice — saying-no is respectful. Stakeholders appreciate more.

P5·PO·5 PO không recap meeting → he-said-she-said PO does not recap meeting → he-said-she-said #

24h Email Recap24h Email Recap👤 PO👤 PO⏱ 10 phút⏱ 10 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
3 tuần sau decision meeting, 2 team nhớ ngược nhau "ai quyết gì". Project block.
3 weeks after the decision meeting, the two teams remembered each other "who decided what". Project block.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Tin "mọi người đã đồng ý" trong head → 3 tuần sau drift → conflict.
News "everyone has agreed" in the head → 3 weeks later drift → conflict.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Quy tắc 24h email recap cho mọi decision meeting: Template (10 phút viết): ``` Subject: Recap — [Topic] decision (Date) Decisions made: • X decided. Owner: [name]. Deadline: [date]. • Y option chosen over Z because [reason]. Action items: • [Person] to do [task] by [date] • [Person] to do [task] by [date] Open questions (need follow-up): • [Question + who responsible] Next meeting: [date if applicable] Reply by [24h] if anything misrepresented. Silence = agreement. ``` Tại sao quan trọng: • Memory drift bắt đầu từ giờ thứ 24. • Stakeholder vacation/đổi role → recap là institutional memory. • Nếu disagreement sau, anh point vào email — fact, không cảm xúc. "Silence = agreement" là dòng quan trọng nhất. Force người không đồng ý phải reply trong 24h, không quietly nuôi disagreement.
Rules24h email recap for every decision meeting:Template(10 minutes of writing): ``` Subject: Recap — [Topic] decision (Date) Decisions made: • X decided. Owner: [name]. Deadline: [date]. • Y option chosen over Z because [reason]. Action items: • [Person] to do [task] by [date] • [Person] to do [task] by [date] Open questions (need follow-up): • [Question + who responsible] Next meeting: [date if applicable] Reply by [24h] if anything misrepresented. Silence = agreement. ```Why is it important?: • Memory drift starts at hour 24. • Stakeholder vacation/role change → recap is institutional memory. • If you disagree later, you point it to the email — facts, not emotions. "Silence = agreement" is the most important line. Force those who disagree to respond within 24 hours, not to quietly harbor dissent.
💡 Lưu ý: Recap discipline = bảo hiểm rẻ nhất chống politics. Build habit từng meeting.💡 Note: Recap discipline = cheapest insurance against politics. Build habit each meeting.
📐 Business Analyst — 5 scripts

P5·BA·1 BA viết status update dài 5 trang, không actionable BA wrote a status update that was 5 pages long, not actionable #

BLUFBLUF📐 BA📐 BA⏱ 10 phút⏱ 10 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
BA viết weekly report 5 trang chi tiết → exec không đọc → action items miss.
BA writes weekly report with 5 detailed pages → exec doesn't read → action items miss.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Nghĩ "viết kỹ = professional" → exec skim 10 giây → mất signal.
Think "write carefully = professional" → exec skim 10 seconds → lose signal.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Em viết kỹ là tốt cho doc internal. Nhưng cho exec/stakeholder, dùng BLUF — Bottom Line Up Front: Cấu trúc (top → bottom): 1. 1 dòng kết luận (BLUF): "Project on track. Demo Q1 ready. 1 risk cần exec input." 2. 3 bullets quan trọng nhất: • Done this week • Risk needing attention • Ask (cụ thể) 3. Detail (optional): link Notion/Confluence cho ai cần. Quy tắc viết: • Câu đầu = trả lời "tôi cần biết gì?" — không setup, không context. • Cut 80% — exec đọc 30 giây, không 5 phút. • Detail có sẵn cho ai dig in. Exec career thrive nhờ skim — không phải họ lười, là vol cao. BLUF tôn trọng thời gian họ.
It's good for internal documents if you write carefully. But for exec/stakeholder, useBLUF — Bottom Line Up Front: Structure(top → bottom): 1.1 line conclusion(BLUF):"Project on track. Demo Q1 ready. 1 risk needs exec input." 2. The 3 most important bullets: • Done this week • Risk relying on attention • Ask (specifically) 3.Details (optional): Notion/Confluence link for those who need it.Writing rules: • First question = answer "what do I need to know?" — no setup, no context. • Cut 80% — exec reads 30 seconds, not 5 minutes. • Details are available for those who dig in. Exec careers thrive thanks to skim — it's not that they're lazy, it's that they have high volume. BLUF respects their time.
💡 Lưu ý: Viết ngắn khó hơn viết dài. Practice: viết detail trước, cut 80% xuống.💡 Note: Writing short is more difficult than writing long. Practice: write details first, cut 80% down.

P5·BA·2 BA không stakeholder map, treat tất cả như nhau BA does not map stakeholders, treat everyone the same #

Power × Interest mapPower × Interest map📐 BA📐 BA⏱ 30 phút⏱ 30 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
BA invite 18 người mọi meeting, gửi update 25 người. Stakeholder khó chịu, exec thì không có signal.
BA invites 18 people to every meeting, sends updates to 25 people. Stakeholder is upset, exec has no signal.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Spam tất cả → exec mute → khi cần ý kiến không ai engaged.
Spam everyone → exec mute → when opinions are needed, no one is engaged.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Mình map stakeholder theo Power × Interest matrix: ``` Low Interest High Interest High Power Keep Satisfied Manage Closely ⭐ priority Low Power Monitor Keep Informed ``` 4 quadrants → 4 chiến lược khác nhau: • Manage Closely (HP/HI): VP, sponsor. Weekly 1:1 + dedicated update. Decision-makers. • Keep Satisfied (HP/LI): senior leaders ngoài project. Monthly summary, không spam weekly. • Keep Informed (LP/HI): users, BA peers. Group update + Slack channel. • Monitor (LP/LI): adjacent teams. Ad-hoc khi relevant. Workshop 30 phút: list 18 stakeholder, map vào quadrant. Sau đó: • Communication plan: ai nhận gì, tần suất nào, channel nào. • Cut spam: KP/LI không cần weekly RAG. KS không cần daily Slack. Tailor message → stakeholder appreciate → engagement tăng.
I map stakeholders accordinglyPower × Interest matrix: ``` Low Interest High Interest High Power Keep Satisfied Manage Closely ⭐ priority Low Power Monitor Keep Informed ``` 4 quadrants → 4 different strategies: •Manage Closely(HP/HI): VP, sponsor. Weekly 1:1 + dedicated updates. Decision-makers. •Keep Satisfied(HP/LI): senior leaders outside the project. Monthly summary, no weekly spam. •Keep Informed(LP/HI): users, THREE peers. Group update + Slack channel. •Monitor(LP/LI): adjacent teams. Ad-hoc when relevant. 30 minute workshop: list 18 stakeholders, map into quadrant. Then: • Communication plan: who receives what, what frequency, what channel. • Cut spam: KP/LI does not need weekly RAG. KS doesn't need daily Slack. Tailor message → stakeholders appreciate → engagement increases.
💡 Lưu ý: Mass communication = no communication. Segment + tailor là kỹ năng PM senior.💡 Note: Mass communication = no communication. Segment + tailor is a senior PM skill.

P5·BA·3 BA giải thích status mơ hồ ("đang ổn") BA explains vague status ("being fine") #

RAG color disciplineRAG color discipline📐 BA📐 BA⏱ 10 phút⏱ 10 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
BA: "Project đang ổn" 4 tuần liền. Tuần 5 đột ngột "🔴 Red". Stakeholder shock.
BA: "Project is going well" for 4 weeks in a row. Week 5 suddenly "🔴 Red". Stakeholder shock.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Default green vì "không muốn lo lắng người ta" → Red đột ngột = mất trust.
Default green because "don't want to worry about people" → Sudden red = loss of trust.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
"Đang ổn" không phải status — là từ chối give status. Anh đặt rule rõ RAG color: 🟢 Green: on track, no major risk in 4 tuần tới. Velocity ổn định, blocker được xử < 24h. 🟡 Amber: 1+ risk có thể impact, mitigation đang in progress. Hoặc velocity dip 1 sprint. Cần monitoring, chưa cần escalation. 🔴 Red: critical blocker, milestone slip risk > 50%. Cần exec action ngay. Rule quan trọng: • Green → Red không bao giờ trực tiếp. Phải qua Amber trước. • Status downgrade (G→A, A→R) phải kèm cause + ask. • Status upgrade (R→A, A→G) phải kèm evidence + measure. Khi có doubt → chọn màu xấu hơn. Stakeholder respect transparency hơn surprise.
"I'm fine" is not a status — it's a refusal to give status. He set clear rulesRAG color: 🟢Green: on track, no major risks in the next 4 weeks. Velocity is stable, blocker is processed in < 24 hours. 🟡Amber: 1+ risk can impact, mitigation is in progress. Or velocity dip 1 sprint.Need monitoring, no need for escalation.🔴Red: critical blocker, milestone slip risk > 50%.Need to execute action immediately. Important rule: • Green → Rednever directly. Must go to Amber first. • Downgrade status (G→A, A→R) must be includedcause + ask. • Status upgrade (R→A, A→G) must be includedevidence + measure. When in doubt → choose the worse color. Stakeholder respect transparency is more surprising.
💡 Lưu ý: Conservative coloring = trust. Optimistic coloring + sudden red = destroy 6 tháng credibility.💡 Note: Conservative coloring = trust. Optimistic coloring + sudden red = destroy 6 months credibility.

P5·BA·4 BA tránh stakeholder difficult BA avoids difficult stakeholders #

Difficult conversation prepDifficult conversation prep📐 BA📐 BA⏱ 30 phút prep⏱ 30 minutes prep
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
BA né stakeholder X (hay critique gay gắt) → X không có context → blow up 1 tháng sau.
BA avoids stakeholder X (or harsh critique) → X has no context → blows up 1 month later.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
"Để sau hỏi" → vấn đề nuôi lớn → khi gặp lại stakeholder, attack mạnh hơn.
"Let's ask later" → raising issues → when meeting stakeholders again, attack harder.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Né stakeholder difficult là feeding the problem. Anh giúp em prep meeting đó: 30 phút trước: 1. What họ care nhất? Risk to job? Reputation? KPI? — đó là lens họ nghe. 2. Anticipate 3 câu hỏi khó: - "Why is this delayed?" - "How do I know this won't happen again?" - "What's your plan?" Em chuẩn bị answer ngắn cho mỗi câu. 3. BLUF của em: 1 câu mở đầu = good news + bad news + ask. Trong meeting: • Dẫn dắt: "Anh có 3 thứ muốn share trong 15 phút: status, 1 risk, và 1 ask cần help anh." • Khi bị critique: không defensive. Acknowledge: "That's fair. Here's what I'm doing about it..." • Em đi ra với 1 ask cụ thể. Sau meeting: email recap trong 24h. Stakeholder difficult thường chỉ test xem em có serious không. 3 lần meeting có chuẩn bị → họ trở thành ally.
Avoid difficult stakeholders is feeding the problem. I'll help you prepare for that meeting:30 minutes ago: 1. What do they care most about? Risk to job? Reputation? KPI? — that's the lens they listen to. 2.Anticipate 3 difficult questions: - "Why is this delayed?" - "How do I know this won't happen again?" - "What's your plan?" I prepare a short answer for each question. 3.My BLUF: 1 opening sentence = good news + bad news + ask.During the meeting: • Leading:"I have 3 things to share in 15 minutes: status, 1 risk, and 1 ask for help."• When being criticized:not defensive. Acknowledge:"That's fair. Here's what I'm doing about it..."• I came out with a specific ask.After the meeting: email recap within 24 hours. Stakeholder difficult usually only tests to see if you are serious. 3 meetings with preparation → they become ally.
💡 Lưu ý: Stakeholder difficult thường ngại stakeholder unprepared. Prep tốt = chuyển dynamic.💡 Note: Stakeholder difficulty often avoids unprepared stakeholders. Good prep = dynamic transfer.

P5·BA·5 BA không escalate concern stakeholder lên cấp BA does not escalate stakeholder concerns #

Escalate-upEscalate-up📐 BA📐 BA⏱ 15 phút⏱ 15 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Stakeholder phàn nàn 3 lần với BA về timeline. BA không lên EM → BA tự cố giải thích → tăng tension.
Stakeholder complained 3 times to BA about the timeline. BA doesn't increase EM → BA tries to explain himself → increases tension.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
BA "ôm" hết → BA stress → quality giảm → stakeholder càng lo.
BA "embraces" everything → BA stress → quality decreases → stakeholders become more worried.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh nhận thấy em đang ôm concern của stakeholder X một mình. Đây là escalation pattern em phải dùng: Escalate up khi nào: • Stakeholder raise issue 2 lần (không phải 1, không phải 3) → ping EM. • Issue ngoài quyền của em (priority, scope, resource) → ping EM ngay lần đầu. • Stakeholder dùng tone hostile/threatening → ping EM ngay. Cách escalate (BLUF format): "@EM — Stakeholder X raised concern lần 2 về timeline epic Y. Họ care vì [reason]. Anh đã reply [recap]. Cần anh: (a) join next meeting với họ, hoặc (b) help me reframe message. Anh chọn?" Anh sẽ làm gì: • Anh không take over your job. • Anh add weight + authority để conversation balance. • Anh có quyền decide trade-off em không có. Em escalate sớm = anh giúp em sớm. Im lặng = anh không biết gì → em chịu trận một mình.
I noticed that you are holding the concern of stakeholder X alone. Here is the escalation pattern you must use:Escalate up when?: • Stakeholder raises issue 2 times (not 1, not 3) → ping EM. • Issue outside your rights (priority, scope, resource) → ping EM the first time. • Stakeholder uses hostile/threatening tone → ping EM immediately.How to escalate (BLUF format): "@EM — Stakeholder What will you do?: • I don't take over your job. • You add weight + authority to balance the conversation. • I have the right to decide trade-off, you don't have. You escalate soon = I will help you soon. Silence = you don't know anything → I'll face the battle alone.
💡 Lưu ý: Escalate ≠ yếu. Đó là dùng đúng resource. EM exists for this.💡 Note: Escalate ≠ weak. That is using the right resources. EM exists for this.
💻 Developer — 5 scripts

P5·DEV·1 Dev không communicate progress, ai cũng phải đi hỏi Dev doesn't communicate progress, everyone has to ask #

Standup commitStandup commit💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 5 phút⏱ 5 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Dev senior chỉ "đang làm" trong standup. Stakeholder, BA, EM phải đi hỏi riêng để biết progress.
Dev senior is just "doing" in standup. Stakeholder, BA, EM have to ask privately to know the progress.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Coi standup là "ritual phiền" → mất chance ngừa misalignment.
Consider standup as "ritual trouble" → lose the chance to prevent misalignment.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Standup không phải để show output cho EM — là tự broadcast tín hiệu để team không phải hỏi em từng người. Format anh muốn em dùng: "Hôm qua: [outcome cụ thể, không phải hoạt động].
Hôm nay: [next concrete step].
Blocker: [có/không + ai own]." Khác biệt: • "Đang làm story X" ❌ — vô nghĩa. • "Hôm qua close PR cho story X, hôm nay merge sau review" ✓ — actionable. 5 dev × 30 giây = 2.5 phút sync. Tiết kiệm: BA 15 phút đi hỏi, EM 15 phút report, stakeholder không panic. ROI 10×. Nếu em cảm thấy "không có gì để báo" 2 ngày liền = signal task em đang làm quá to / blocker ẩn → mình tách story.
Standup is not to show output to EM — it isautomatically broadcast the signal so that the team doesn't have to ask me each person. Format I want you to use:"Yesterday: [specific output, not activity].
Today: [next concrete step].
Blocker: [yes/no + who owns]." Distinctive: • "Working on story X" ❌ — meaningless. • "Yesterday closed PR for story X, today merged after review" ✓ — actionable. 5 dev × 30 seconds = 2.5 minutes sync. Savings: BA 15 minutes to ask, EM 15 minutes to report, stakeholders don't panic. 10× ROI. If you feel like you have "nothing to report" for 2 days in a row = signal the task you're doing is too big / hidden blocker → I split the story.
💡 Lưu ý: Standup format = team contract. EM enforce nhẹ nhàng nhưng nhất quán.💡 Note: Standup format = team contract. EM enforces gently but consistently.

P5·DEV·2 Dev defensive khi stakeholder challenge Dev is defensive when stakeholders challenge #

Stakeholder empathyStakeholder empathy💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 15 phút coach⏱ 15 minutes coaching
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Trong demo, stakeholder hỏi "Why feature X chậm thế?" — Dev defensive: "Vì PO thay scope".
During the demo, stakeholders asked "Why is feature X so slow?" — Dev defensive: "Because PO replaces scope."
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Defensive + blame PO trước stakeholder → stakeholder mất confidence + PO mất face.
Defensive + blame PO in front of stakeholders → stakeholder loses confidence + PO loses face.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh thấy lúc đó căng. Cho em framework cho lần tới — không phải để giả vờ vui: 3 reflexes thay defensive: 1. Acknowledge concern trước: "Cảm ơn anh đã raise — câu hỏi đúng." (5 giây. Cho phép em reset bộ não.) 2. Trả lời bằng "what" và "next", không "why": - "Hiện tại: feature X 70%, expected delivery sprint sau." - "Plan: anh demo lại Friday tuần sau với trạng thái update." Tránh đi ngược tìm "ai có lỗi". Stakeholder không care, họ care path forward. 3. Nếu thật sự có ngoại lực (PO scope change): - "Có scope change tuần 3 — mình đang work với PO để ngừa lặp lại." - Không name-blame trước stakeholder. Sau meeting: nếu PO thật sự gây delay, anh + em + PO bàn 1-1 — không public.
I felt tense at that moment. Give me a framework for next time — not just to pretend to be happy:3 reflexes replace defensive: 1. Acknowledge concerns first: "Thank you for raising — correct question."(5 seconds. Allow me to reset my brain.) 2.Answer with "what" and "next", not "why": - "Currently: feature X 70%, expected delivery sprint later." - "Plan: I'll demo again next Friday with updated status."Avoid going backwards looking for "who is at fault". Stakeholders don't care, they care path forward. 3.If there really is an external force (PO scope change): - "There is a scope change in week 3 — I'm working with PO to prevent repetition."- Do not name-blame before stakeholders.After the meeting: If PO really causes delay, you + me + PO discuss 1-1 — not public.
💡 Lưu ý: Public blame phá team. Chỉ EM xử politics offline. Dev focus solution-forward language.💡 Note: Public blame destroys the team. Only EM handles politics offline. Dev focus solution-forward language.

P5·DEV·3 Dev jargon-heavy với stakeholder không tech Dev jargon-heavy with non-tech stakeholders #

Translate technicalTranslate technical💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 10 phút⏱ 10 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Dev demo: "Mình refactor service layer, swap ORM, optimize N+1 query, cache với Redis". Stakeholder: 😶.
Dev demo: "I refactor service layer, swap ORM, optimize N+1 query, cache with Redis". Stakeholders: 😶.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Stakeholder gật gù không hiểu → không tin team → ngại approve roadmap technical.
Stakeholder nodded in confusion → did not trust the team → was afraid to approve the technical roadmap.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Em làm tech tốt. Nhưng nếu stakeholder không hiểu, work của em invisible với họ. Trick translate: Quy tắc 3 layers: 1. Outcome cho user (1 câu, ngôn ngữ của họ): "Trang load nhanh gấp 3 — từ 2.5s xuống 0.8s." 2. Business impact (nếu relevant): "Theo benchmark: cho mỗi 1s nhanh = +5% conversion → est ~$30k/tháng." 3. Technical detail (chỉ khi họ hỏi): "Mình cache database query phổ biến với Redis — chi tiết sẵn cho team kỹ thuật xem." Tip nhỏ: • Trước demo, hỏi anh: "audience là ai?" → anh nói tech-level cần dùng. • Tránh acronym (ORM, N+1, IaC...) — replace bằng plain English. • Dùng metaphor: "ORM giống như translator giữa code và database." Communication skill này cũng là career skill — senior engineer giỏi translate được.
I'm good at tech. But if stakeholders don't understand, your work is invisible to them. Trick translation:3 layers rule: 1. Outcome for users(1 sentence, their language):"Page loads 3 times faster — from 2.5 seconds to 0.8 seconds." 2. Business impact(if relevant):"According to benchmark: for each 1 second fast = +5% conversion → est ~$30k/month." 3. Technical details(only if they ask):"I cache a common database query with Redis — details are available for the technical team to see." Small tip: • Before the demo, ask him: "Who is Audience?" → he said tech-level is needed. • Avoid acronyms (ORM, N+1, IaC...) — replace with plain English. • Use metaphor: "ORM is like a translator between code and database." This communication skill is also a career skill — senior engineers are good at translating.
💡 Lưu ý: "Jargon = chứng tỏ" là anti-pattern. Plain language với business impact = senior signal.💡 Note:"Jargon = prove" is anti-pattern. Plain language with business impact = senior signal.

P5·DEV·4 Dev không attend stakeholder demo Dev does not attend stakeholder demo #

Demo as career investmentDemo as career investment💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 10 phút⏱ 10 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Dev: "Demo dài, em không cần đi, em là người code, BA demo cũng được". Sau release stakeholder không biết ai trong team build cái gì.
Dev: "The demo is long, you don't need to go, I'm a coder, THREE demos are fine." After release stakeholders do not know who in the team is building what.

EM đồng ý → dev invisible → không có visibility → khó promote.
I agree → dev invisible → no visibility → difficult to promote.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh hiểu logic em — nhưng demo là career investment, không waste: 3 lý do em nên đi: 1. Visibility: stakeholder thấy em là người build → khi promo committee bàn, có name recognition. Em không show up = ai cũng giả định BA hoặc EM build hết. 2. Direct feedback loop: em nghe stakeholder phản ứng real-time với cái em build. Sau filter qua BA/EM, signal mất 60%. 3. Skill: present technical work cho non-tech audience là skill — luyện càng sớm càng tốt cho senior path. Đề xuất compromise: • Em demo phần em build (10 phút) — không cần đứng cả buổi. • Anh prep với em trước (15 phút coach về translate-technical). • Sau demo, em có thể leave nếu không relevant. Mỗi sprint em demo 1 lần = 6 lần/quarter visibility. Đó là career capital.
I understand your logic — but demo iscareer investment, no waste: 3 reasons you should go: 1.Visibility: stakeholders see that you are a builder → when promoting the committee table, there is name recognition. I don't show up = everyone assumes that BA or EM built it all. 2.Direct feedback loop: I heard stakeholders reactreal-timewith what I built. After filtering through BA/EM, the signal is lost 60%. 3.Skills: present technical work for non-tech audience is a skill — practice as early as possible for the senior path.Propose compromise: • I demo the part I build (10 minutes) — no need to stand the whole time. • I prep with you first (15 minutes coaching on translation-technical). • After the demo, you can leave if it's not relevant. Each sprint I demo 1 time = 6 times/quarter visibility. That's career capital.

P5·DEV·5 Dev escalate qua Slack DM thay vì proper channel Dev escalates via Slack DM instead of proper channel #

Escalation routingEscalation routing💻 Dev💻 Dev⏱ 10 phút⏱ 10 minutes
📍 Bối cảnh📍 Background
Dev DM EM lúc 9pm về tech disagreement với team khác. Nhanh nhưng không có context cho team, không tracked.
Dev DM EM at 9pm about tech disagreement with another team. Fast but no context for the team, no tracking.
❌ Sai lầm❌ Mistake
Reply DM → chỉ 2 người biết → repeat issue lần sau với người khác.
Reply DM → only 2 people know → repeat issue next time with others.
✅ Script✅ Scripts
Anh thấy em DM về vụ với team API — anh sẵn sàng giúp, nhưng dạy em routing đúng: Escalation channel ladder: 1. Tech disagreement → public channel #tech-discussion với context. Có người ngoài team có insight. 2. Cross-team blocker (technical) → ping tech lead + EM trong #project-{name}, public. Có audit trail. 3. Cross-team blocker (priority/scope) → ping EM + PO. EM có quyền escalate director nếu cần. 4. Personal/sensitive (1-1 issue, conflict cá nhân) → DM EM. Đây mới là DM legitimate. Lý do tránh DM cho technical: • Knowledge mất — không ai tham gia learning. • Decision không tracked → repeat. • EM trở thành single point of failure cho mọi answer. Thread công khai có vẻ "ồn ào" nhưng healthier cho team long-term. Vụ API team — em paste lại trong #project, anh reply trên đó cho cả team thấy.
I saw you DM about the matter with the API team — I'm willing to help, but teach you correct routing:Escalation channel ladder: 1. Tech dissent→ public channel#tech-discussionwith context. Someone outside the team has insight. 2.Cross-team blocker (technical)→ ping tech lead + EM in#project-{name}, public. There is an audit trail. 3.Cross-team blocker (priority/scope)→ ping EM + PO. EM has the right to escalate director if necessary. 4.Personal/sensitive(1-1 issue, personal conflict) → DM EM. This is legitimate DM.Reasons to avoid DMs for technical purposes: • Knowledge is lost — no one participates in learning. • Decision not tracked → repeat. • EM becomes the single point of failure for every answer. Public threads seem "noisy" but healthier for long-term teams. API team case — I'll paste it here#project, he replied there for the whole team to see.
💡 Lưu ý: Default-public communication. DM chỉ cho thực sự sensitive. Build culture từ đầu.💡 Note: Default-public communication. DM only for the truly sensitive. Build culture from scratch.
